/**

import { DurableObject } from "cloudflare:workers";



// 1. CONSTANTS & SECURITY

const GOSPEL = { PURPLE: "#a855f7", GOLD: "#f59e0b", DARK: "#0f0f1a" };

const CORS = {

"Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",

"Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, GET, OPTIONS",

"Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, x-sovereign-key, x-chat-key"

};



// 2. THE GATE (WORKER)

export default {

async fetch(request, env) {

const url = new URL(request.url);


// PREFLIGHT CHECK

if (request.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response(null, { headers: CORS });



// ROUTER

try {

// A. HEALTH CHECK (Public)

if (url.pathname === "/health") {

return new Response(JSON.stringify({

status: "ONLINE",

version: env.PHOENIX_VERSION,

gospel: 444

}), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...CORS } });

}



// B. MAGIC CHAT UI (The Throne Room)

if (url.pathname === "/admin/chat") {

return handleAdminMagicChat(request, env);

}



// C. EXECUTE/LEDGER (Auth Required)

if (url.pathname === "/execute" || url.pathname === "/ledger/append") {

return await env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME")).fetch(request);

}



return new Response("PHOENIX GATE: 404", { status: 404, headers: CORS });



} catch (e) {

return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: CORS });

}

}

};



// 3. THE UI (GOSPEL 444 COMMAND STATION)

function handleAdminMagicChat(req, env) {

const html = `

<!DOCTYPE html>

<html>

<head>

body { background-color: ${GOSPEL.DARK}; color: ${GOSPEL.GOLD}; font-family: monospace; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; height: 100vh; margin: 0; }

<p>VERSION: v100.5-DIAMOND</p>

<p>TARGET: 1,000,000,000 VND</p>

<div class="status">STATUS: ONLINE</div>

</div>

</body>

</html>`;


return new Response(html, { headers: { "Content-Type": "text/html" } });

}



// 4. DURABLE OBJECTS (THE AUTHORITY)



// THE LEDGER (Heart)

export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 extends DurableObject {

// THE SESSION STORE (Memory)

}

}



// THE MEMORY (Context)

async fetch(){ return new Response("OK"); }

}

name = "phoenix-rising-protocol"

main = "src/index.js"

compatibility_date = "2024-04-01"

# 🦅 DURABLE OBJECTS (REALITY C BINDINGS)

name = "LEDGER"

[[durable_objects.bindings]]

name = "SESSIONS"

class_name = "PhoenixSessionStore"



[[durable_objects.bindings]]

name = "RATELIMITER"

[[durable_objects.bindings]]

name = "MEMORY"

class_name = "PhoenixMemoryDO"



# 🦅 MIGRATIONS (THE HISTORY)

tag = "v1-diamond-genesis"

new_classes = ["PhoenixLedgerDOv2", "PhoenixSessionStore", "RateLimiter", "PhoenixMemoryDO"]



# 🦅 MOVE 72: THE PURGE

# We formally delete the ghosts. If Cloudflare complains they don't exist, remove the specific class from this list.

tag = "v2-move72-purge"

"PhoenixLedgerDO",

"PhoenixOmegaDO",

"PhoenixOmegaDO_v2"

]



[vars]

PHOENIX_VERSION = "v100.6-MOVE72"

GOSPEL = "444"

/**

* 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA — REALITY C MONOLITH v100.6 (MOVE 72 EDITION)

* ==============================================================

* AUTHORITY: AGENT-100 Michael Hobbs (The Sovereign)

* STATUS: FAIL-CLOSED | ZERO-DRIFT | GOSPEL 444

*/



import { DurableObject } from "cloudflare:workers";



// 1. CONSTANTS & SECURITY

const GOSPEL = { PURPLE: "#a855f7", GOLD: "#f59e0b", DARK: "#0f0f1a" };

const CORS = {

"Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",

"Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, GET, OPTIONS",

"Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, x-sovereign-key, x-chat-key"

};



// 2. THE GATE (WORKER)

export default {

async fetch(request, env) {

const url = new URL(request.url);

if (request.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response(null, { headers: CORS });



try {

// HEALTH CHECK (Public)

if (url.pathname === "/health") {

return new Response(JSON.stringify({

status: "ONLINE",

version: env.PHOENIX_VERSION,

gospel: 444,

checkpoint_cadence: env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE

}), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...CORS } });

}



// ROUTER

if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append") {

// Enforce Sovereign Auth

const key = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key");

// <PLACEHOLDER: AUTH_CHECK> (In prod, use crypto.subtle.timingSafeEqual)

// For now, fail-closed if missing.

if (!key) return new Response("ACCESS_DENIED", { status: 401, headers: CORS });


return await env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME")).fetch(request);

}



return new Response("PHOENIX GATE: 404", { status: 404, headers: CORS });



} catch (e) {

return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: CORS });

}

}

};



// 3. THE AUTHORITY (PHOENIX LEDGER DO v2)

export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 extends DurableObject {

constructor(state, env) {

super(state, env);

this.state = state;

this.env = env;

}



async fetch(request) {

// 1. Parse Input

const data = await request.json();


// 2. Lock State

let height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0;

let prevHash = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";


const newHeight = height + 1;

const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();


// 3. MOVE 72: CHECKPOINT LOGIC

let type = "STANDARD_BLOCK";

const cadence = parseInt(this.env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE || "100");

if (newHeight % cadence === 0) {

type = "CHECKPOINT_BLOCK";

// Inject Policy Hash to prove Law hasn't drifted

data.policy_verification = this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED";

}



// 4. Commit

const payload = JSON.stringify(data);

const newHash = "HASH_" + newHeight + "_" + timestamp; // <PLACEHOLDER: REAL_SHA256_FUNC>



await this.state.storage.put("height", newHeight);

await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", newHash);


return new Response(JSON.stringify({

status: "COMMITTED",

height: newHeight,

type: type,

hash: newHash

}), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });

}

}



// 4. SUPPORT SYSTEMS (Sessions, RateLimit, Memory)

export class PhoenixSessionStore extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK");} }

export class RateLimiter extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK")} }

export class PhoenixMemoryDO extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK")} }

import { DurableObject } from "cloudflare:workers";



// 1. CONSTANTS & GOSPEL

const GOSPEL = { PURPLE: "#a855f7", GOLD: "#f59e0b", DARK: "#0f0f1a" };

const CHECKPOINT_CADENCE = 100; // Every 100 blocks

const POLICY_HASH = "PLACEHOLDER_POLICY_HASH"; // Will compute dynamically if JSON provided



// 2. CRYPTO PRIMITIVES (SHA-256)

async function sha256(str) {

const buf = new TextEncoder().encode(str);

const hash = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", buf);

return Array.from(new Uint8Array(hash)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');

}



// 3. THE LEDGER (PhoenixLedgerDOv2)

export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 extends DurableObject {

constructor(state, env) {

this.state = state;

this.sql = state.storage.sql;


// Atomic Boot: Ensure Table Exists

this.state.blockConcurrencyWhile(async () => {

this.sql.exec(`

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ledger (

id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

hash TEXT NOT NULL,

prev_hash TEXT NOT NULL,

payload TEXT NOT NULL,

timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,

checkpoint_hash TEXT -- The Move 72 Anchor

);

`);

});

}



async fetch(req) {

// ... (Auth Logic Here) ...



if (req.url.endsWith("/append")) {

const body = await req.json();


// MOVE 72 LOGIC: CHECKPOINT HASHING

const isCheckpoint = (currentHeight + 1) % CHECKPOINT_CADENCE === 0;

const checkpointVal = isCheckpoint ? POLICY_HASH : null;



// ... (Insert Logic with checkpointVal) ...


return new Response(JSON.stringify({ status: "APPENDED", height: currentHeight + 1, checkpoint: isCheckpoint }));

}


return new Response("PHOENIX LEDGER ONLINE", { status: 200 });

}

}

/**

* 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA — REALITY C MONOLITH v100.6 (MOVE 72 EDITION)

* ==============================================================

* AUTHORITY: AGENT-100 Michael Hobbs (The Sovereign)

* STATUS: FAIL-CLOSED | ZERO-DRIFT | GOSPEL 444

import { DurableObject } from "cloudflare:workers";



// 1. CONSTANTS & SECURITY

const GOSPEL = { PURPLE: "#a855f7", GOLD: "#f59e0b", DARK: "#0f0f1a" };

const CORS = {

"Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",

"Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, GET, OPTIONS",

"Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, x-sovereign-key, x-chat-key"

};



// 2. THE GATE (WORKER)

export default {

async fetch(request, env) {

const url = new URL(request.url);

if (request.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response(null, { headers: CORS });



try {

// HEALTH CHECK (Public)

if (url.pathname === "/health") {

return new Response(JSON.stringify({

status: "ONLINE",

version: env.PHOENIX_VERSION,

gospel: 444,

checkpoint_cadence: env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE

}), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...CORS } });

}



// ROUTER

if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append") {

const key = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key");

// <PLACEHOLDER: AUTH_CHECK> (Fail-closed if missing)

if (!key) return new Response("ACCESS_DENIED", { status: 401, headers: CORS });


return await env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME")).fetch(request);

}



return new Response("PHOENIX GATE: 404", { status: 404, headers: CORS });



} catch (e) {

return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: CORS });

}

}

};



// 3. THE AUTHORITY (PHOENIX LEDGER DO v2)

export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 extends DurableObject {

constructor(state, env) {

super(state, env);

this.state = state;

this.env = env;

}



async fetch(request) {

// 1. Parse Input

const data = await request.json();


// 2. Lock State

let height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0;

let prevHash = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";


const newHeight = height + 1;

const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();


// 3. MOVE 72: CHECKPOINT LOGIC

// Enforces the Law at fixed intervals

let type = "STANDARD_BLOCK";

const cadence = parseInt(this.env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE || "100");

if (newHeight % cadence === 0) {

type = "CHECKPOINT_BLOCK";

// Inject Policy Hash to prove Law hasn't drifted

data.policy_verification = this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED";

}



// 4. Commit (Simulated Hash for Monolith)

// <PLACEHOLDER: REAL_SHA256_FUNC>

const newHash = "HASH_" + newHeight + "_" + timestamp;



await this.state.storage.put("height", newHeight);

await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", newHash);


return new Response(JSON.stringify({

status: "COMMITTED",

height: newHeight,

type: type,

hash: newHash

}), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });

}

}



// 4. SUPPORT SYSTEMS (Sessions, RateLimit, Memory)

export class PhoenixSessionStore extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK");} }

export class RateLimiter extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK")} }

export class PhoenixMemoryDO extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK")} }

/**

* 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA — REALITY C MONOLITH v100.6 (MOVE 72 FAIL-SOFT)

* ================================================================

* AUTHORITY: AGENT-100 Michael Hobbs

* STATUS: FAIL-CLOSED | GOSPEL 444

*/



import { DurableObject } from "cloudflare:workers";



// 1. CRYPTO PRIMITIVES (Safe + Consistent)

async function sha256Hex(str) {

const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(str));

return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");

}



// 2. THE GATE (WORKER)

export default {

async fetch(request, env) {

if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {

return new Response(null, { headers: { "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*" } });

}

const url = new URL(request.url);



try {

// HEALTH CHECK

if (url.pathname === "/health") {

return new Response(JSON.stringify({

status: "ONLINE",

version: env.PHOENIX_VERSION,

gospel: 444,

mode: "FAIL-CLOSED"

}), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });

}



// LEDGER ACCESS (AUTH REQUIRED)

if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" || url.pathname === "/verifychain") {

const key = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key");

if (!key) return new Response("ACCESS_DENIED", { status: 401 });



const id = env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME");

return await env.LEDGER.get(id).fetch(request);

}



return new Response("PHOENIX GATE: 404", { status: 404 });

} catch (e) {

return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message }), { status: 500 });

}

}

};



// 3. THE AUTHORITY (Active PhoenixLedgerDOv2)

export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 extends DurableObject {

constructor(state, env) {

super(state, env);

this.state = state;

this.env = env;

}



async fetch(request) {

const url = new URL(request.url);



// VERIFY CHAIN (Read)

if (url.pathname === "/verifychain") {

let height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0;

let head_hash = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";

return new Response(JSON.stringify({

ok: true,

stone_sky: true,

height: height,

head_hash: head_hash

}), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });

}



// APPEND (Write)

if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" || request.method === "POST") {

const data = await request.json();

let height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0;

let prevHash = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";



const newHeight = height + 1;

const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();



// CHECKPOINT LOGIC

const cadence = parseInt(this.env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE || "100");

if (newHeight % cadence === 0) {

data.checkpoint_hash = this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED";

data.type = "CHECKPOINT";

}



// HASHING

const payloadStr = JSON.stringify(data);

const newHash = await sha256Hex(`${newHeight}:${prevHash}:${timestamp}:${payloadStr}`);



await this.state.storage.put("height", newHeight);

await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", newHash);

await this.state.storage.put(`block_${newHeight}`, { ts: timestamp, hash: newHash, data });



return new Response(JSON.stringify({

status: "COMMITTED",

height: newHeight,

hash: newHash

}), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });

}

return new Response("DO OK");

}

}



// 4. SUPPORT SYSTEMS (Active Exports)

export class PhoenixSessionStore extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK");} }

export class RateLimiter extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK")} }

export class PhoenixMemoryDO extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK")} }



// 5. GHOST STUBS (THE SAFETY NET)

// We export these so Cloudflare sees them present during the deletion migration.

export class PhoenixLedgerZenith extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("PURGED",{status:410})} }

// 2. CRYPTO PRIMITIVES

async function sha256(str) {

const buf = new TextEncoder().encode(str);

const hash = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", buf);

return Array.from(new Uint8Array(hash)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');

}

async fetch(req) {

if (req.url.endsWith("/append")) {

const body = await req.json();


// MOVE 72 LOGIC: CHECKPOINT HASHING

// We read the current height to determine if this is a checkpoint block.

const countResult = this.sql.exec("SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM ledger").toArray();

const currentHeight = countResult.length ? countResult.c : 0;

const nextHeight = currentHeight + 1;


// If (Height % 100 == 0), we inject the Policy Hash.

const isCheckpoint = nextHeight % CHECKPOINT_CADENCE === 0;

const checkpointVal = isCheckpoint ? POLICY_HASH : null;



// ... (Standard Insert Logic would go here, including checkpointVal) ...


return new Response(JSON.stringify({

status: "APPENDED",

height: nextHeight,

checkpoint: isCheckpoint,

policy_sealed: !!checkpointVal

}), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });

}

return new Response("PHOENIX LEDGER ONLINE", { status: 200 });

}

}

Here’s the pipeline layout (10 files, no extras):



src/00_consts.js

src/01_http.js (cors/json/text + helpers)

src/02_auth.js (timingSafeEq + requireKey)

src/03_crypto.js (sha256Hex + nowISO + mustInt)

src/04_rate_limiter_do.js (RateLimiter DO)

src/05_ledger_do.js (PhoenixLedgerDOv2 DO)

src/06_drones.js (DroneSwarm)

src/07_chat.js (chatProcess + local fallback + later OBI wiring)

src/08_routes.js (router table + protectedPaths + rate-limit gate)

src/index.js (imports + exports default + DO exports)

export const VERSION_DEFAULT = "UNSET";

export const GOSPEL_DEFAULT = "444";

export const CORS_ALLOW_HEADERS = "Content-Type, x-sovereign-key, x-chat-key";

# 2) http

"use strict";

import { CORS_ALLOW_HEADERS, CORS_ALLOW_METHODS, CORS_MAX_AGE } from "./00_consts.js";



export function cors(origin = "*") {

return {

"Access-Control-Allow-Origin": origin,

"Access-Control-Allow-Methods": CORS_ALLOW_METHODS,

"Access-Control-Allow-Headers": CORS_ALLOW_HEADERS,

"Access-Control-Max-Age": CORS_MAX_AGE,

"Vary": "Origin",

};

}



export function json(body, status = 200, extraHeaders = {}) {

return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {

status,

headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8", ...extraHeaders },

});

}



export function text(body, status = 200, extraHeaders = {}) {

return new Response(body, {

status,

headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", ...extraHeaders },

});

}

JS



# 3) auth

cat > src/p10/02_auth.js <<'JS'

"use strict";



export function timingSafeEq(a, b) {

if (typeof a !== "string" || typeof b !== "string") return false;

if (a.length !== b.length) return false;

if (a.length === 0) return false;

let out = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) out |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);

return out === 0;

}



export function requireKey(req, env) {

const got = req.headers.get("x-sovereign-key") || "";

const expected = (env.SOVEREIGN_KEY || "").trim();

if (!expected) return false;

if (!got) return false;

return timingSafeEq(got, expected);

}

JS



# 4) crypto/time/int

cat > src/p10/03_crypto.js <<'JS'

"use strict";

export function mustInt(v, fallback) {

const n = Number.parseInt(String(v ?? ""), 10);

return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;

}

export function nowISO() { return new Date().toISOString(); }



export async function sha256Hex(str) {

const buf = new TextEncoder().encode(str);

const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", buf);

return [...new Uint8Array(digest)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");

}

JS



# 5) rate limiter DO

cat > src/p10/04_rate_limiter_do.js <<'JS'

"use strict";

import { json, text } from "./01_http.js";

import { mustInt } from "./03_crypto.js";



export class RateLimiter {

constructor(state, env) { this.state = state; this.env = env; }



async fetch(req) {

const url = new URL(req.url);

if (url.pathname !== "/check") return text("NOT_FOUND", 404);



const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);

const key = String(body?.key || "default");

const cost = mustInt(body?.cost, 1);



const cap = mustInt(this.env.RL_CAPACITY, 60);

const refillPerSec = mustInt(this.env.RL_REFILL_PER_SEC, 1);



const now = Date.now();

const k = `rl:${key}`;

const rec = (await this.state.storage.get(k)) || { tokens: cap, ts: now };



const elapsed = Math.max(0, (now - rec.ts) / 1000);

const refill = Math.floor(elapsed * refillPerSec);

const tokens = Math.min(cap, rec.tokens + refill);



if (tokens < cost) {

await this.state.storage.put(k, { tokens, ts: now });

return json({ ok: false, error: "RATE_LIMIT", tokens, cap }, 429);

}



const next = tokens - cost;

await this.state.storage.put(k, { tokens: next, ts: now });

return json({ ok: true, tokens: next, cap }, 200);

}

}

JS



# 6) ledger DO

cat > src/p10/05_ledger_do.js <<'JS'

"use strict";

import { json, text } from "./01_http.js";

import { mustInt, nowISO, sha256Hex } from "./03_crypto.js";



export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 {

constructor(state, env) {

this.state = state;

this.env = env;

this.sql = state.storage.sql;



this.state.blockConcurrencyWhile(async () => {

this.sql.exec(`

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ledger (

height INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,

hash TEXT NOT NULL,

prev_hash TEXT NOT NULL,

payload TEXT NOT NULL,

ts TEXT NOT NULL,

type TEXT NOT NULL,

policy_hash TEXT

);

`);



const h = await this.state.storage.get("height");

if (typeof h !== "number") await this.state.storage.put("height", 0);

const head = await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash");

if (typeof head !== "string") await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", "GENESIS");

});

}



async fetch(req) {

const url = new URL(req.url);



if (url.pathname === "/verifychain" && req.method === "GET") {

const height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0;

const head = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";

return json({

ok: true,

stone_sky: true,

height,

head_hash: head,

policy_hash: String(this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED"),

version: this.env.PHOENIX_VERSION || "UNSET",

});

}



if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" && req.method === "POST") {

const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);

if (!body || typeof body !== "object") return text("BAD_JSON", 400);



const cadence = mustInt(this.env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE, 100);

const policyHash = String(this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED");



const height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0;

const prev = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";

const next = height + 1;



const ts = nowISO();

const checkpoint = cadence > 0 && next % cadence === 0;

const type = checkpoint ? "CHECKPOINT" : "STANDARD";

const payloadObj = checkpoint ? { ...body, policy_verification: policyHash, type: "CHECKPOINT" } : { ...body };

const payload = JSON.stringify(payloadObj);

const hash = await sha256Hex(`${next}:${prev}:${ts}:${payload}`);



this.sql.exec(

"INSERT INTO ledger (height, hash, prev_hash, payload, ts, type, policy_hash) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?);",

next, hash, prev, payload, ts, type, checkpoint ? policyHash : null

);



await this.state.storage.put("height", next);

await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", hash);



return json({ ok: true, status: "COMMITTED", height: next, hash, type, checkpoint });

}



return text("DO_NOT_FOUND", 404);

}

}

JS



# 7) drones

"use strict";

import { mustInt } from "./03_crypto.js";



export class DroneSwarm {

constructor() { this.counter = 0; this.drones = []; }



spawn(count = 1) {

const n = Math.max(0, Math.min(999, mustInt(count, 1)));

const now = Date.now();

for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {

this.counter += 1;

const id = String(this.counter).padStart(3, "0");

const cls = ["SPY", "GUARDIAN", "COURIER"][this.counter % 3];

this.drones.push({ id: `DRONE-${id}`, class: cls, tier: "PRIMORDIAL", spawned: now, lifespan: 3600000, status: "ACTIVE" });

}

return this.drones.slice(-n);

}



status() {

const alive = this.drones.filter((d) => d.status === "ACTIVE").length;

return { total: this.drones.length, alive, drones: this.drones.slice(-50) };

}

}

"use strict";



function hasUpstreamKeys(env) {

return (

Boolean((env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY || "").trim()) ||

Boolean((env.OPENAI_API_KEY || "").trim()) ||

Boolean((env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY || "").trim()) ||

Boolean((env.GEMINI_API_KEY || "").trim()) ||

Boolean((env.GROK_API_KEY || "").trim()) ||

Boolean((env.DEEPSEEK_API_KEY || "").trim())

);

}



export function chatProcess(message, env) {

const m = String(message ?? "").trim();

if (!m) return { response: "", source: "LOCAL", error: "EMPTY_MESSAGE" };

if (!hasUpstreamKeys(env)) return { response: `LOCAL_MODE_OK: ${m}`, source: "LOCAL" };



// Later: wire your OBI orchestrator here.

}

# 9) routes

cat > src/p10/08_routes.js <<'JS'

"use strict";

import { cors, json, text } from "./01_http.js";

import { requireKey } from "./02_auth.js";

import { mustInt } from "./03_crypto.js";

import { LEDGER_NAME, RL_GLOBAL_NAME, VERSION_DEFAULT, GOSPEL_DEFAULT } from "./00_consts.js";

import { chatProcess } from "./07_chat.js";



export function makeRouter(deps) {

const { droneSwarm } = deps;



return async function route(req, env) {

const url = new URL(req.url);



if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: cors("*") });



if (url.pathname === "/health" && req.method === "GET") {

return json({

ok: true,

status: "OPERATIONAL",

version: env.PHOENIX_VERSION || VERSION_DEFAULT,

gospel: env.GOSPEL || GOSPEL_DEFAULT,

policy_hash: env.POLICY_HASH || "UNSET",

ledger_bound: Boolean(env.LEDGER),

rate_limiter_bound: Boolean(env.RATE_LIMITER),

}, 200, cors("*"));

}



if (url.pathname === "/chat" && req.method === "POST") {

const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));

const out = chatProcess(body?.message, env);

const status = out.error ? 400 : 200;

return json({ response: out.response ?? "", source: out.source ?? "LOCAL", error: out.error }, status, cors("*"));

}



if (url.pathname === "/drones/status" && req.method === "GET") {

return json(droneSwarm.status(), 200, cors("*"));

}



if (url.pathname === "/drones/spawn" && req.method === "POST") {

const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));

const spawned = droneSwarm.spawn(mustInt(body?.count, 99));

return json({ spawned: spawned.length, active: spawned.slice(0, 10) }, 200, cors("*"));

}



// protected

const protectedPaths = new Set(["/verifychain", "/ledger/append"]);

if (protectedPaths.has(url.pathname) && !requireKey(req, env)) return text("ACCESS_DENIED", 401, cors("*"));



// optional RL gate on append

if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append") {

const useRL = String(env.RL_ENABLED || "1") !== "0";

if (useRL && env.RATE_LIMITER) {

const rlId = env.RATE_LIMITER.idFromName(RL_GLOBAL_NAME);

const rlRes = await env.RATE_LIMITER.get(rlId).fetch("https://rl/check", {

method: "POST",

body: JSON.stringify({ key: "ledger_append", cost: 1 }),

});

if (!rlRes.ok) return new Response(await rlRes.text(), { status: rlRes.status, headers: cors("*") });

}

}



if (url.pathname === "/verifychain" && req.method === "GET") {

const id = env.LEDGER.idFromName(LEDGER_NAME);

return env.LEDGER.get(id).fetch(req);

}



if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" && req.method === "POST") {

const id = env.LEDGER.idFromName(LEDGER_NAME);

return env.LEDGER.get(id).fetch(req);

}



return text("NOT_FOUND", 404, cors("*"));

};

}

JS

cat > src/index.js <<'JS'

"use strict";

import { DroneSwarm } from "./p10/06_drones.js";

import { makeRouter } from "./p10/08_routes.js";

export { RateLimiter } from "./p10/04_rate_limiter_do.js";

export { PhoenixLedgerDOv2 } from "./p10/05_ledger_do.js";



const droneSwarm = new DroneSwarm();

const router = makeRouter({ droneSwarm });



export default {

async fetch(req, env, ctx) {

return router(req, env, ctx);

}

};

1. Production-ready hardened src/index.js (pure law)javascript

// FILE: src/index.js

"use strict";



/**

* Phoenix Rising Protocol — Hardened Worker + Durable Objects

* - Fail-closed auth on protected endpoints

* - Ledger DO (SQLite) with checkpoint anchoring + SHA-256 chain

* - /health (public), /verifychain (auth), /ledger/append (auth)

* - Optional RateLimiter DO (token bucket)

*/



function json(body, status = 200, extraHeaders = {}) {

return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {

status,

headers: {

"Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8",

...extraHeaders,

},

});

}



function text(body, status = 200, extraHeaders = {}) {

return new Response(body, {

status,

headers: {

"Content-Type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8",

...extraHeaders,

},

});

}



function cors(origin = "*") {

return {

"Access-Control-Allow-Origin": origin,

"Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET,POST,OPTIONS",

"Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, x-sovereign-key, x-chat-key",

"Access-Control-Max-Age": "86400",

"Vary": "Origin",

};

}



function requireKey(req, env) {

const got = req.headers.get("x-sovereign-key") || "";

const expected = (env.SOVEREIGN_KEY || "").trim();



// Fail-closed: if no expected key is configured, treat as locked.

if (!expected) return false;

if (!got) return false;

return timingSafeEq(got, expected);

}



// constant-time-ish compare (guards simple timing leaks); handles empty strings safely

function timingSafeEq(a, b) {

if (typeof a !== "string" || typeof b !== "string") return false;

if (a.length !== b.length) return false;

if (a.length === 0) return false;

let out = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) out |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);

return out === 0;

}



async function sha256Hex(str) {

const buf = new TextEncoder().encode(str);

const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", buf);

return [...new Uint8Array(digest)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");

}



function mustInt(str, fallback) {

const n = Number.parseInt(String(str ?? ""), 10);

return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;

}



function nowISO() {

return new Date().toISOString();

}



export default {

async fetch(request, env, ctx) {

const url = new URL(request.url);



// CORS preflight

if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {

return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: cors("*") });

}



// Public health

if (url.pathname === "/health" && request.method === "GET") {

return json(

{

ok: true,

status: "ONLINE",

version: env.PHOENIX_VERSION || "UNSET",

gospel: env.GOSPEL || "444",

checkpoint_cadence: env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE || "100",

},

200,

cors("*")

);

}



// Protected endpoints

const protectedPaths = new Set(["/verifychain", "/ledger/append"]);

if (protectedPaths.has(url.pathname)) {

if (!requireKey(request, env)) {

return text("ACCESS_DENIED", 401, cors("*"));

}

}



// Optional rate limit gate (recommended for /ledger/append)

if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append") {

const useRL = String(env.RL_ENABLED || "1") !== "0";

if (useRL && env.RATELIMITER) {

const rlId = env.RATELIMITER.idFromName("GLOBAL");

const rlRes = await env.RATELIMITER.get(rlId).fetch("https://rl/check", {

method: "POST",

body: JSON.stringify({

key: "ledger_append",

cost: 1,

}),

});

if (!rlRes.ok) {

const payload = await rlRes.text();

return new Response(payload, { status: rlRes.status, headers: cors("*") });

}

}

}



// Route into Ledger DO (singleton)

if (url.pathname === "/verifychain" && request.method === "GET") {

const id = env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME");

return env.LEDGER.get(id).fetch(request);

}



if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" && request.method === "POST") {

const id = env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME");

return env.LEDGER.get(id).fetch(request);

}



return text("NOT_FOUND", 404, cors("*"));

},

};



// -------------------- Durable Objects --------------------



export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 {

constructor(state, env) {

this.state = state;

this.env = env;

this.sql = state.storage.sql;



// Boot: create schema once

this.state.blockConcurrencyWhile(async () => {

this.sql.exec(`

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ledger (

height INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,

hash TEXT NOT NULL,

prev_hash TEXT NOT NULL,

payload TEXT NOT NULL,

ts TEXT NOT NULL,

type TEXT NOT NULL,

policy_hash TEXT

);

`);



// Ensure metadata row exists via storage (not SQL) for fast reads

const h = await this.state.storage.get("height");

if (typeof h !== "number") await this.state.storage.put("height", 0);

const head = await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash");

if (typeof head !== "string") await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", "GENESIS");

});

}



async fetch(request) {

const url = new URL(request.url);



if (url.pathname === "/verifychain" && request.method === "GET") {

const height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0;

const headHash = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";

const policyHash = String(this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED");

return json({

ok: true,

stone_sky: true,

height,

head_hash: headHash,

policy_hash: policyHash,

version: this.env.PHOENIX_VERSION || "UNSET",

});

}



if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" && request.method === "POST") {

const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);

if (!body || typeof body !== "object") return text("BAD_JSON", 400);



const cadence = mustInt(this.env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE, 100);

const policyHash = String(this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED");



// Atomic-ish append (single-threaded per DO, still guard against partial writes)

const height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0;

const prevHash = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";

const nextHeight = height + 1;



const ts = nowISO();

const isCheckpoint = cadence > 0 && nextHeight % cadence === 0;



const type = isCheckpoint ? "CHECKPOINT" : "STANDARD";

const payloadObj = isCheckpoint

? { ...body, policy_verification: policyHash, type: "CHECKPOINT" }

: { ...body };



const payload = JSON.stringify(payloadObj);

const newHash = await sha256Hex(`${nextHeight}:${prevHash}:${ts}:${payload}`);



// Write SQL row

this.sql.exec(

"INSERT INTO ledger (height, hash, prev_hash, payload, ts, type, policy_hash) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?);",

nextHeight,

newHash,

prevHash,

payload,

ts,

type,

isCheckpoint ? policyHash : null

);



// Update fast head pointers

await this.state.storage.put("height", nextHeight);

await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", newHash);



return json({

ok: true,

status: "COMMITTED",

height: nextHeight,

hash: newHash,

type,

checkpoint: isCheckpoint,

});

}



return text("DO_NOT_FOUND", 404);

}

}



export class PhoenixSessionStore {

constructor(state, env) {

this.state = state;

this.env = env;

}

async fetch() {

return text("OK", 200);

}

}



export class PhoenixMemoryDO {

constructor(state, env) {

this.state = state;

this.env = env;

}

async fetch() {

return text("OK", 200);

}

}



// Simple token-bucket rate limiter (DO-scoped)

export class RateLimiter {

constructor(state, env) {

this.state = state;

this.env = env;

}



async fetch(request) {

const url = new URL(request.url);

if (url.pathname !== "/check") return text("NOT_FOUND", 404);



const body = await request.json().catch(() => null);

const key = String(body?.key || "default");

const cost = mustInt(body?.cost, 1);



const cap = mustInt(this.env.RL_CAPACITY, 60);

const refillPerSec = mustInt(this.env.RL_REFILL_PER_SEC, 1);



const now = Date.now();

const recKey = `rl:${key}`;



const rec =

(await this.state.storage.get(recKey)) ||

({ tokens: cap, ts: now } /* seed */);



const elapsedSec = Math.max(0, (now - rec.ts) / 1000);

const refill = Math.floor(elapsedSec * refillPerSec);

const tokens = Math.min(cap, rec.tokens + refill);



if (tokens < cost) {

await this.state.storage.put(recKey, { tokens, ts: now });

return json({ ok: false, error: "RATE_LIMIT", tokens, cap }, 429);

}



const next = tokens - cost;

await this.state.storage.put(recKey, { tokens: next, ts: now });

return json({ ok: true, tokens: next, cap }, 200);

}

}

3. Revenue Engine Autonomy Spec (scale to 1B VND)



Core objective: convert inbound signals (leads, payments, student actions) into deterministic ledger events with strict idempotency, bounded retries, and auditable outcomes.



A. Event model (immutable)



* Every revenue-relevant action is an event: LEAD_INGESTED, TIGER_SCORED, INVOICE_CREATED, PAYMENT_CONFIRMED, REFUND_ISSUED, CONTRACT_RENEWED.

* Each event includes:



* event_id (UUID or deterministic hash)

* actor_id (lead/student/operator)

* amount_vnd (0 allowed for non-monetary)

* source (manychat/webhook/manual)

* timestamp

* idempotency_key (required for anything that can be retried)



B. Idempotency (non-negotiable)



* Idempotency key format: "<source>:<external_id>:<event_type>".

* Ledger enforces “at most once” per idempotency key:



* Store a seen:<idempotency_key> -> committed_height/hash index (in DO storage or SQL unique index).

* If key exists: return the prior commit response (do not append a new block).



C. CAS / retry policy (bounded)



* If using a compare-and-swap pattern (or you detect head changed mid-flow), retry up to N=3.

* Backoff: 0ms / 25ms / 75ms, then fail closed with an explicit error that can be re-queued.

* Never infinite loops. Never “best effort” silent success.



D. Autonomy guardrails



* Budget guard: per-event cost cap; if exceeded, degrade to “queue for review”.

* TMST guard: any autonomous task must produce either:



* a ledger state mutation, or

* a durable “queued outcome”

within 600 seconds, else it is killed and marked TIMEOUT_ABORTED.

* Rate limiting:



* Global and per-actor throttles (token bucket).

* Hard 429, never 500.



E. Scaling architecture (recommended)



* Ingest Worker: validates signatures + normalizes events + emits to queue (or directly calls Ledger if low volume).

* Queue/Buffer (if volume spikes): batches events, preserves ordering per actor.

* Ledger DO: single-writer authoritative chain (your PhoenixLedgerDOv2).

* Auditor DO/Cron: periodic verification:



* head continuity

* checkpoint cadence compliance

* policy hash matches configured expected policy hash

* anomaly detection (duplicate payments, negative totals, sudden spikes)



F. “1B VND” progress computation (stable)



* Do not compute progress by scanning entire chain per request.

* Maintain rolling aggregates in DO storage:



* revenue_total_vnd

* revenue_by_day

* revenue_by_source

* Update aggregates only on append commit, derived from the event payload.



G. Failure modes (explicit)



* ACCESS_DENIED (401)

* RATE_LIMIT (429)

* DUPLICATE_EVENT (200 with prior receipt)

* BAD_EVENT (400)

* INTERNAL_ERROR (500) — should be rare; treat as incident.



H. Observability



* Log correlation id: request_id.

* Emit structured logs for:



* event accepted/rejected

* idempotency hit

* append commit receipt (height/hash)

* retry attempts

* checkpoint creation

// FILE: PHOENIX_SINGULARITY_MONOLITH.js

"use strict";



const CORS_HEADERS = {

"Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",

"Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET,POST,OPTIONS",

"Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, x-sovereign-key, x-chat-key",

"Access-Control-Max-Age": "86400",

"Vary": "Origin",

};



const json = (x, status = 200, h = {}) =>

new Response(JSON.stringify(x), { status, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8", ...h } });



const text = (s, status = 200, h = {}) =>

new Response(s, { status, headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", ...h } });



const timingSafeEq = (a, b) => {

if (typeof a !== "string" || typeof b !== "string") return false;

if (a.length !== b.length) return false;

if (a.length === 0) return false;

let out = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) out |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i);

return out === 0;

};



const requireKey = (req, env) => {

const got = req.headers.get("x-sovereign-key") || "";

const expected = (env.SOVEREIGN_KEY || "").trim();

if (!expected) return false;

if (!got) return false;

return timingSafeEq(got, expected);

};



const mustInt = (v, fb) => {

const n = Number.parseInt(String(v ?? ""), 10);

return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fb;

};



const sha256Hex = async (str) => {

const buf = new TextEncoder().encode(str);

const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", buf);

return [...new Uint8Array(digest)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");

};



const nowISO = () => new Date().toISOString();



export default {

async fetch(req, env) {

const url = new URL(req.url);

if (req.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: CORS_HEADERS });



if (url.pathname === "/health" && req.method === "GET") {

return json(

{

ok: true,

status: "ONLINE",

version: env.PHOENIX_VERSION || "UNSET",

gospel: env.GOSPEL || "444",

checkpoint_cadence: env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE || "100",

},

200,

CORS_HEADERS

);

}



const protectedPaths = new Set(["/verifychain", "/ledger/append"]);

if (protectedPaths.has(url.pathname) && !requireKey(req, env)) return text("ACCESS_DENIED", 401, CORS_HEADERS);



if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" && String(env.RL_ENABLED || "1") !== "0" && env.RATELIMITER) {

const rlId = env.RATELIMITER.idFromName("GLOBAL");

const r = await env.RATELIMITER.get(rlId).fetch("https://rl/check", {

method: "POST",

body: JSON.stringify({ key: "ledger_append", cost: 1 }),

});

if (!r.ok) return new Response(await r.text(), { status: r.status, headers: CORS_HEADERS });

}



if (url.pathname === "/verifychain" && req.method === "GET") {

const id = env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME");

return env.LEDGER.get(id).fetch(req);

}



if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" && req.method === "POST") {

const id = env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME");

return env.LEDGER.get(id).fetch(req);

}



return text("NOT_FOUND", 404, CORS_HEADERS);

},

};

const policyHash = String(this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED");

const prev = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";

const next = height + 1;

const ts = nowISO();

const checkpoint = cadence > 0 && next % cadence === 0;

const type = checkpoint ? "CHECKPOINT" : "STANDARD";

const payloadObj = checkpoint ? { ...body, policy_verification: policyHash, type: "CHECKPOINT" } : { ...body };

const payload = JSON.stringify(payloadObj);

const hash = await sha256Hex(`${next}:${prev}:${ts}:${payload}`);



this.sql.exec(

"INSERT INTO ledger (height, hash, prev_hash, payload, ts, type, policy_hash) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?);",

next,

hash,

prev,

payload,

ts,

type,

checkpoint ? policyHash : null

);



await this.state.storage.put("height", next);

await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", hash);



return json({ ok: true, status: "COMMITTED", height: next, hash, type, checkpoint });

}



return text("DO_NOT_FOUND", 404);

}

}

=== exports + DO classes in src/index.js ===

import { ChatEndpoint } from './chat_endpoint.js';

import { DroneSwarm } from './drone_swarm.js';



const droneSwarm = new DroneSwarm();



export default {

async fetch(request, env, ctx) {

const url = new URL(request.url);



const corsHeaders = {

'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',

'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET, POST, OPTIONS',

'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type',

};



if (request.method === 'OPTIONS') {

return new Response(null, { headers: corsHeaders });

}



if (url.pathname === '/chat' && request.method === 'POST') {

const chat = new ChatEndpoint();

const response = await chat.handle(request, env);

return new Response(response.body, {

headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }

});

}



if (url.pathname === '/drones/spawn' && request.method === 'POST') {

const { count } = await request.json();

const drones = droneSwarm.spawn(count || 99);

return Response.json({ spawned: drones.length, active: drones.slice(0, 10) }, { headers: corsHeaders });

}



if (url.pathname === '/drones/status') {

return Response.json(droneSwarm.status(), { headers: corsHeaders });

}



if (url.pathname === '/health') {

return Response.json({

status: 'OPERATIONAL',

stages: 120,

agents: {

'100': 'SOVEREIGN',

'99': env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY ? 'ONLINE' : 'OFFLINE',

'98': env.OPENAI_API_KEY ? 'ONLINE' : 'OFFLINE',

'97': env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, '98B': env.GEMINI_API_KEY ? 'ONLINE' : 'OFFLINE'

},

version: 'CANON-v1.0'

}, { headers: corsHeaders });

}



return new Response('Phoenix Empire - Agent Hierarchy Active', {

status: 200,

headers: corsHeaders

});

}

};



export class RateLimiter {

constructor(state, env) { this.state = state; }

import { ChatEndpoint } from './chat_endpoint.js';

import { DroneSwarm } from './drone_swarm.js';



const droneSwarm = new DroneSwarm();



export default {

async fetch(request, env, ctx) {

const url = new URL(request.url);



const corsHeaders = {

'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',

'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET, POST, OPTIONS',

'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type',

};



if (request.method === 'OPTIONS') {

return new Response(null, { headers: corsHeaders });

}



if (url.pathname === '/chat' && request.method === 'POST') {

const chat = new ChatEndpoint();

const response = await chat.handle(request, env);

return new Response(response.body, {

headers: { ...corsHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }

});

}



if (url.pathname === '/drones/spawn' && request.method === 'POST') {

const { count } = await request.json();

const drones = droneSwarm.spawn(count || 99);

return Response.json({ spawned: drones.length, active: drones.slice(0, 10) }, { headers: corsHeaders });

}



if (url.pathname === '/drones/status') {

return Response.json(droneSwarm.status(), { headers: corsHeaders });

}



if (url.pathname === '/health') {

return Response.json({

status: 'OPERATIONAL',

stages: 120,

agents: {

'100': 'SOVEREIGN',

'99': env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY ? 'ONLINE' : 'OFFLINE',

'98': env.OPENAI_API_KEY ? 'ONLINE' : 'OFFLINE',

'97': env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, '98B': env.GEMINI_API_KEY ? 'ONLINE' : 'OFFLINE'

},

version: 'CANON-v1.0'

}, { headers: corsHeaders });

}



return new Response('Phoenix Empire - Agent Hierarchy Active', {

status: 200,

headers: corsHeaders

});

}

};



export class RateLimiter {

constructor(state, env) { this.state = state; }

async fetch(request) { return new Response("OK", { status: 200 }); }

}

} // Rate limit recommended on append if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" && request.method === "POST") { const useRL = String(env.RL_ENABLED || "1") !== "0"; if (useRL && env.RATE_LIMITER) { const rlId = env.RATE_LIMITER.idFromName("GLOBAL"); } if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" && request.method === "POST") { const id = env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME"); return env.LEDGER.get(id).fetch(request); } return text("NOT_FOUND", 404, cors("*")); }, }; /* -------------------- Durable Objects -------------------- */ // SQLite ledger DO export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 { constructor(state, env) { this.state = state; this.env = env; this.sql = state.storage.sql; this.state.blockConcurrencyWhile(async () => { this.sql.exec(` CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ledger ( height INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, hash TEXT NOT NULL, prev_hash TEXT NOT NULL, payload TEXT NOT NULL, ts TEXT NOT NULL, type TEXT NOT NULL, policy_hash TEXT ); `); const h = await this.state.storage.get("height"); if (typeof h !== "number") await this.state.storage.put("height", 0); const head = await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash"); if (typeof head !== "string") await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", "GENESIS"); }); } async fetch(request) { const url = new URL(request.url); if (url.pathname === "/verifychain" && request.method === "GET") { const height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0; const headHash = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS"; const policyHash = String(this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED"); return json({ ok: true, stone_sky: true, height, head_hash: headHash, policy_hash: policyHash, version: this.env.PHOENIX_VERSION || "UNSET", }); const policyHash = String(this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED"); const height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0; const prevHash = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS"; const nextHeight = height + 1; const ts = nowISO(); const isCheckpoint = cadence > 0 && nextHeight % cadence === 0; const type = isCheckpoint ? "CHECKPOINT" : "STANDARD"; const payloadObj = isCheckpoint ? { ...body, policy_verification: policyHash, type: "CHECKPOINT" } : { ...body }; const payload = JSON.stringify(payloadObj); const newHash = await sha256Hex(`${nextHeight}:${prevHash}:${ts}:${payload}`); this.sql.exec( "INSERT INTO ledger (height, hash, prev_hash, payload, ts, type, policy_hash) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?);", nextHeight, newHash, prevHash, payload, ts, type, isCheckpoint ? policyHash : null ); await this.state.storage.put("height", nextHeight); await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", newHash); return json({ ok: true, status: "COMMITTED", height: nextHeight, hash: newHash, type, checkpoint: isCheckpoint, }); } return text("DO_NOT_FOUND", 404); } } // Token-bucket limiter (DO storage, not SQLite) export class RateLimiter { constructor(state, env) { this.state = state; this.env = env; } async fetch(request) { const url = new URL(request.url); if (url.pathname !== "/check") return text("NOT_FOUND", 404); const body = await request.json().catch(() => null); const key = String(body?.key || "default"); const cost = mustInt(body?.cost, 1); const cap = mustInt(this.env.RL_CAPACITY, 60); const refillPerSec = mustInt(this.env.RL_REFILL_PER_SEC, 1); const now = Date.now(); const recKey = `rl:${key}`; const rec = (await this.state.storage.get(recKey)) || { tokens: cap, ts: now }; const elapsedSec = Math.max(0, (now - rec.ts) / 1000); const refill = Math.floor(elapsedSec * refillPerSec); const tokens = Math.min(cap, rec.tokens + refill); if (tokens < cost) { await this.state.storage.put(recKey, { tokens, ts: now }); return json({ ok: false, error: "RATE_LIMIT", tokens, cap }, 429); } const next = tokens - cost; await this.state.storage.put(recKey, { tokens: next, ts: now }); return json({ ok: true, tokens: next, cap }, 200); } }

"CHECKPOINT" : "STANDARD"; const payloadObj = isCheckpoint ? { ...body, policy_verification: policyHash, type: "CHECKPOINT" } : { ...body }; const payload = JSON.stringify(payloadObj); const newHash = await sha256Hex(`${nextHeight}:${prevHash}:${ts}:${payload}`); this.sql.exec( "INSERT INTO ledger (height, hash, prev_hash, payload, ts, type, policy_hash) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?);", nextHeight, newHash, prevHash, payload, ts, type, isCheckpoint ? policyHash : null ); await this.state.storage.put("height", nextHeight); await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", newHash); return json({ ok: true, status: "COMMITTED", height: nextHeight, hash: newHash, type, checkpoint: isCheckpoint, }); } return text("DO_NOT_FOUND", 404); } } export class RateLimiter { constructor(state, env) { this.state = state; this.env = env; } async fetch(request) { const url = new URL(request.url); if (url.pathname !== "/check") return text("NOT_FOUND", 404); const body = await request.json().catch(() => null); const key = String(body?.key || "default"); const cost = mustInt(body?.cost, 1); const cap = mustInt(this.env.RL_CAPACITY, 60); const refillPerSec = mustInt(this.env.RL_REFILL_PER_SEC, 1); const now = Date.now(); const recKey = `rl:${key}`; const rec = (await this.state.storage.get(recKey)) || { tokens: cap, ts: now }; const elapsedSec = Math.max(0, (now - rec.ts) / 1000); const refill = Math.floor(elapsedSec * refillPerSec); const tokens = Math.min(cap, rec.tokens + refill); if (tokens < cost) { await this.state.storage.put(recKey, { tokens, ts: now }); return json({ ok: false, error: "RATE_LIMIT", tokens, cap }, 429); } const next = tokens - cost; await this.state.storage.put(recKey, { tokens: next, ts: now }); return json({ ok: true, tokens: next, cap }, 200); } } JS echo "=== sanity ===" node -e "require('fs').readFileSync('src/index.js','utf8'); console.log('read_ok')" grep -nE 'export default|export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2|export class RateLimiter' src/index.js

We fix this the simplest way: create .dev.vars, restart Wrangler, re-run the auth tests.

Step 1 — create .dev.vars (in the worker directory)

Run:



cd ~/phoenix-empire/worker cat > .dev.vars <<'VARS' SOVEREIGN_KEY=dev-sovereign-key-change-me PHOENIX_VERSION=v100.6-JUDGMENT-LAYER CHECKPOINT_CADENCE=100 RL_ENABLED=1 RL_CAPACITY=10 RL_REFILL_PER_SEC=1 POLICY_HASH=DEV_UNVERIFIED VARS ls -la .dev.vars sed -n '1,20p' .dev.vars

Step 2 — restart local dev so Wrangler loads the vars

In the Wrangler terminal:

press x or Ctrl+C to stop

Then run:



cd ~/phoenix-empire/worker npx wrangler dev --local --persist-to .wrangler/state

Step 3 — re-run the contract tests

In the second terminal:



export WORKER_URL="http://127.0.0.1:8787" export SOVEREIGN_KEY="dev-sovereign-key-change-me" curl -sS "$WORKER_URL/health" curl -i -sS "$WORKER_URL/verifychain" | head -n 10 curl -sS "$WORKER_URL/verifychain" -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOVEREIGN_KEY" curl -sS "$WORKER_URL/ledger/append" -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOVEREIGN_KEY" -H "Content-Type: application/json" --data '{"event":"local_test_append","ts":"now"}' curl -sS "$WORKER_URL/verifychain" -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOVEREIGN_KEY"

Paste the auth verifychain output (the JSON). Once we see stone_sky: true and height increments, we immediately test Magic Chat /chat and drones to ensure the merge didn’t break the command station.

You’re fine. Two separate things happened 🙂

First: that “huh?” line is just you echoing the tail end of the script creation in the terminal. It doesn’t mean anything is wrong.

Second (the real thing): your new ./dev.local.sh started on http://localhost:8788 instead of 8787 because 8787 was already in use by another running Wrangler/dev server. Wrangler auto-bumped the port. ✅

Do this now:

Confirm what’s using 8787 and decide whether to kill it



ss -ltnp | grep ':8787' || true ss -ltnp | grep ':8788' || true

If you want only ONE dev server, kill the old one:



pkill -f "wrangler dev" || true

Then restart:



cd ~/phoenix-empire/worker ./dev.local.sh

Update your test URL to match the active port (8788 right now)

In the other terminal:



export WORKER_URL="http://127.0.0.1:8788" export SOVEREIGN_KEY="dev-sovereign-key-change-me" curl -sS "$WORKER_URL/health" curl -sS "$WORKER_URL/verifychain" -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOVEREIGN_KEY"

If you want to force a fixed port every time, set it explicitly

Edit dev.local.sh to include --port 8787:



cd ~/phoenix-empire/worker sed -i 's/npx wrangler dev /npx wrangler dev --port 8787 /' dev.local.sh

But note: it will fail if something is already on 8787, so the better pattern is “kill old wrangler then start”.

Also: --var POLICY_HASH:DEV_UNVERIFIED is just setting the ledger’s “policy hash” label for dev. It’s not security; it’s a trace tag. In production you’ll set it to your real policy hash (or remove it until you implement policy verification).

// FILE: src/index.js "use strict"; /** * PHOENIX WORKER MONOLITH 100 (single file) * - Routes: /health, /chat, /drones/status, /drones/spawn, /verifychain, /ledger/append, /stages, /stage/run * - Durable Objects: PhoenixLedgerDOv2 (SQLite), RateLimiter (token bucket) * - Auth: fail-closed x-sovereign-key for protected routes * - Chat: deterministic LOCAL_MODE fallback if no upstream vendor keys configured * - Stages: Registry 001..100 with safe stubs; run via /stage/run (auth) */ /* -------------------- utilities -------------------- */ function cors(origin = "*") { return { "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": origin, "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "GET,POST,OPTIONS", "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, x-sovereign-key, x-chat-key", "Access-Control-Max-Age": "86400", "Vary": "Origin", }; } function json(body, status = 200, extraHeaders = {}) { return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=utf-8", ...extraHeaders, }, }); } function text(body, status = 200, extraHeaders = {}) { return new Response(body, { status, headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", ...extraHeaders, }, }); } // constant-time-ish compare (guards simple timing leaks); handles empty strings safely function timingSafeEq(a, b) { if (typeof a !== "string" || typeof b !== "string") return false; if (a.length !== b.length) return false; if (a.length === 0) return false; let out = 0; for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) out |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i); return out === 0; } function requireKey(req, env) { const got = req.headers.get("x-sovereign-key") || ""; const expected = (env.SOVEREIGN_KEY || "").trim(); if (!expected) return false; // fail-closed if unset if (!got) return false; return timingSafeEq(got, expected); } function mustInt(v, fallback) { const n = Number.parseInt(String(v ?? ""), 10); return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback; } function nowISO() { return new Date().toISOString(); } async function sha256Hex(str) { const buf = new TextEncoder().encode(str); const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", buf); return [...new Uint8Array(digest)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join(""); } /* -------------------- in-memory drones (dev-friendly) -------------------- */ class DroneSwarm { constructor() { this.counter = 0; this.drones = []; } spawn(count = 1) { const n = Math.max(0, Math.min(999, mustInt(count, 1))); const now = Date.now(); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { this.counter += 1; const id = String(this.counter).padStart(3, "0"); const cls = ["SPY", "GUARDIAN", "COURIER"][this.counter % 3]; this.drones.push({ id: `DRONE-${id}`, class: cls, tier: "PRIMORDIAL", spawned: now, lifespan: 3600000, status: "ACTIVE", }); } return this.drones.slice(-n); } status() { const alive = this.drones.filter((d) => d.status === "ACTIVE").length; return { total: this.drones.length, alive, drones: this.drones.slice(-50) }; } } const droneSwarm = new DroneSwarm(); /* -------------------- stage system 001..100 -------------------- */ function stageId(n) { const x = mustInt(n, 0); return String(x).padStart(3, "0"); } function makeStage(n, name, fn) { return { id: stageId(n), n, name, // fn(ctx) => { ok, ... } (must be deterministic + bounded) run: fn || (async () => ({ ok: true, status: "STUB", note: "unimplemented" })), }; } // A few “real” examples; the rest are safe stubs you can fill later. const STAGES = {}; STAGES["001"] = makeStage(1, "BOOT_HEALTHCHECK", async (ctx) => { return { ok: true, status: "OK", ts: nowISO(), version: ctx.env.PHOENIX_VERSION || "UNSET" }; }); STAGES["002"] = makeStage(2, "LEDGER_VERIFY", async (ctx) => { const id = ctx.env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME"); const res = await ctx.env.LEDGER.get(id).fetch("https://ledger/verifychain", { method: "GET", headers: ctx.headers }); const data = await res.json().catch(() => null); return { ok: Boolean(data?.ok), verify: data }; }); STAGES["003"] = makeStage(3, "LEDGER_APPEND_TEST", async (ctx) => { const id = ctx.env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME"); const res = await ctx.env.LEDGER.get(id).fetch("https://ledger/ledger/append", { method: "POST", headers: { ...ctx.headers, "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ event: "stage_003_append", ts: nowISO() }), }); const data = await res.json().catch(() => null); return { ok: Boolean(data?.ok), append: data }; }); STAGES["004"] = makeStage(4, "DRONES_SMOKE", async () => { const spawned = droneSwarm.spawn(3); return { ok: true, spawned: spawned.length, sample: spawned }; }); STAGES["005"] = makeStage(5, "CHAT_LOCAL_SMOKE", async (ctx) => { const message = "hello"; const out = chatProcess(message, ctx.env); return { ok: true, response: out.response, source: out.source }; }); // Fill stubs 006..100 for (let n = 6; n <= 100; n++) { const id = stageId(n); if (!STAGES[id]) STAGES[id] = makeStage(n, `STAGE_${id}`, null); } async function runStageById(id, ctx) { const s = STAGES[id]; if (!s) return { ok: false, error: "NO_SUCH_STAGE", id }; const t0 = Date.now(); const out = await s.run(ctx); const ms = Date.now() - t0; return { ok: Boolean(out?.ok), id: s.id, name: s.name, ms, result: out }; } /* -------------------- chat (deterministic local fallback) -------------------- */ function hasUpstreamKeys(env) { return ( Boolean((env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY || "").trim()) || Boolean((env.OPENAI_API_KEY || "").trim()) || Boolean((env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY || "").trim()) || Boolean((env.GEMINI_API_KEY || "").trim()) || Boolean((env.GROK_API_KEY || "").trim()) || Boolean((env.DEEPSEEK_API_KEY || "").trim()) ); } // Keep this pure/deterministic. If you later wire vendor connectors, do it here. function chatProcess(message, env) { const m = String(message ?? "").trim(); if (!m) return { response: "", source: "LOCAL", error: "EMPTY_MESSAGE" }; if (!hasUpstreamKeys(env)) { return { response: `LOCAL_MODE_OK: ${m}`, source: "LOCAL" }; } // Placeholder: upstream orchestration not embedded in this monolith by default. // You can swap this for your OBIOrchestrator later. return { response: `UPSTREAM_KEYS_PRESENT_BUT_NOT_WIRED: ${m}`, source: "LOCAL" }; } /* -------------------- worker routes -------------------- */ export default { async fetch(request, env, ctx) { const url = new URL(request.url); if (request.method === "OPTIONS") { return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: cors("*") }); } // Public health if (url.pathname === "/health" && request.method === "GET") { return json( { ok: true, status: "OPERATIONAL", stages: 100, version: env.PHOENIX_VERSION || "UNSET", policy_hash: env.POLICY_HASH || "UNSET", ledger_bound: Boolean(env.LEDGER), rate_limiter_bound: Boolean(env.RATE_LIMITER), agents: { "100": "SOVEREIGN", "99": (env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY || "").trim() ? "ONLINE" : "OFFLINE", "98": (env.OPENAI_API_KEY || "").trim() ? "ONLINE" : "OFFLINE", "97": (env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY || "").trim() ? "ONLINE" : "OFFLINE", "98B": (env.GEMINI_API_KEY || "").trim() ? "ONLINE" : "OFFLINE", }, }, 200, cors("*") ); } // Public chat if (url.pathname === "/chat" && request.method === "POST") { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const message = body?.message; const out = chatProcess(message, env); const status = out.error ? 400 : 200; return json({ response: out.response ?? "", source: out.source ?? "LOCAL", error: out.error }, status, cors("*")); } // Public drones if (url.pathname === "/drones/status" && request.method === "GET") { return json(droneSwarm.status(), 200, cors("*")); } if (url.pathname === "/drones/spawn" && request.method === "POST") { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const count = mustInt(body?.count, 99); const spawned = droneSwarm.spawn(count); return json({ spawned: spawned.length, active: spawned.slice(0, 10) }, 200, cors("*")); } // Protected routes const protectedPaths = new Set(["/verifychain", "/ledger/append", "/stages", "/stage/run"]); if (protectedPaths.has(url.pathname)) { if (!requireKey(request, env)) return text("ACCESS_DENIED", 401, cors("*")); } // Optional rate limit gate (recommended for /ledger/append) if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append") { const useRL = String(env.RL_ENABLED || "1") !== "0"; if (useRL && env.RATE_LIMITER) { const rlId = env.RATE_LIMITER.idFromName("GLOBAL"); const rlRes = await env.RATE_LIMITER.get(rlId).fetch("https://rl/check", { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ key: "ledger_append", cost: 1 }), }); if (!rlRes.ok) return new Response(await rlRes.text(), { status: rlRes.status, headers: cors("*") }); } } // Route into Ledger DO (singleton) if (url.pathname === "/verifychain" && request.method === "GET") { const id = env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME"); return env.LEDGER.get(id).fetch(request); } if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" && request.method === "POST") { const id = env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME"); return env.LEDGER.get(id).fetch(request); } // Stage list + stage run if (url.pathname === "/stages" && request.method === "GET") { const list = Object.values(STAGES) .sort((a, b) => a.n - b.n) .map((s) => ({ id: s.id, n: s.n, name: s.name })); return json({ ok: true, count: list.length, stages: list }, 200, cors("*")); } if (url.pathname === "/stage/run" && request.method === "POST") { const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const id = String(body?.id ?? "").trim(); if (!id) return json({ ok: false, error: "MISSING_STAGE_ID" }, 400, cors("*")); const stage = STAGES[id]; if (!stage) return json({ ok: false, error: "NO_SUCH_STAGE", id }, 404, cors("*")); const ctxObj = { env, headers: { "x-sovereign-key": request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key") || "" }, }; // bounded execution budget const out = await runStageById(id, ctxObj); return json(out, 200, cors("*")); } return text("NOT_FOUND", 404, cors("*")); }, }; /* -------------------- Durable Objects -------------------- */ export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 { constructor(state, env) { this.state = state; this.env = env; this.sql = state.storage.sql; this.state.blockConcurrencyWhile(async () => { this.sql.exec(` CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ledger ( height INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, hash TEXT NOT NULL, prev_hash TEXT NOT NULL, payload TEXT NOT NULL, ts TEXT NOT NULL, type TEXT NOT NULL, policy_hash TEXT ); `); const h = await this.state.storage.get("height"); if (typeof h !== "number") await this.state.storage.put("height", 0); const head = await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash"); if (typeof head !== "string") await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", "GENESIS"); }); } async fetch(request) { const url = new URL(request.url); if (url.pathname === "/verifychain" && request.method === "GET") { const height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0; const headHash = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS"; const policyHash = String(this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED"); return json({ ok: true, stone_sky: true, height, head_hash: headHash, policy_hash: policyHash, version: this.env.PHOENIX_VERSION || "UNSET", }); } if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" && request.method === "POST") { const body = await request.json().catch(() => null); if (!body || typeof body !== "object") return text("BAD_JSON", 400); const cadence = mustInt(this.env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE, 100); const policyHash = String(this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED"); const height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0; const prevHash = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS"; const nextHeight = height + 1; const ts = nowISO(); const isCheckpoint = cadence > 0 && nextHeight % cadence === 0; const type = isCheckpoint ? "CHECKPOINT" : "STANDARD"; const payloadObj = isCheckpoint ? { ...body, policy_verification: policyHash, type: "CHECKPOINT" } : { ...body }; const payload = JSON.stringify(payloadObj); const newHash = await sha256Hex(`${nextHeight}:${prevHash}:${ts}:${payload}`); this.sql.exec( "INSERT INTO ledger (height, hash, prev_hash, payload, ts, type, policy_hash) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?);", nextHeight, newHash, prevHash, payload, ts, type, isCheckpoint ? policyHash : null ); await this.state.storage.put("height", nextHeight); await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", newHash); return json({ ok: true, status: "COMMITTED", height: nextHeight, hash: newHash, type, checkpoint: isCheckpoint, }); } return text("DO_NOT_FOUND", 404); } } // Simple token-bucket rate limiter (DO-scoped) export class RateLimiter { constructor(state, env) { this.state = state; this.env = env; } async fetch(request) { const url = new URL(request.url); if (url.pathname !== "/check") return text("NOT_FOUND", 404); const body = await request.json().catch(() => null); const key = String(body?.key || "default"); const cost = mustInt(body?.cost, 1); const cap = mustInt(this.env.RL_CAPACITY, 60); const refillPerSec = mustInt(this.env.RL_REFILL_PER_SEC, 1); const now = Date.now(); const recKey = `rl:${key}`; const rec = (await this.state.storage.get(recKey)) || { tokens: cap, ts: now }; const elapsedSec = Math.max(0, (now - rec.ts) / 1000); const refill = Math.floor(elapsedSec * refillPerSec); const tokens = Math.min(cap, rec.tokens + refill); if (tokens < cost) { await this.state.storage.put(recKey, { tokens, ts: now }); return json({ ok: false, error: "RATE_LIMIT", tokens, cap }, 429); } const next = tokens - cost; await this.state.storage.put(recKey, { tokens: next, ts: now }); return json({ ok: true, tokens: next, cap }, 200); } }

Yes. Let’s shrink it to a 10-script pipeline so you can iterate without monolith pain 🙂✅

Goal: keep src/index.js lean, and split the system into exactly 10 modules you can adjust independently, while preserving your working endpoints and DOs.

Here’s the pipeline layout (10 files, no extras):

src/00_consts.js

src/01_http.js (cors/json/text + helpers)

src/02_auth.js (timingSafeEq + requireKey)

src/03_crypto.js (sha256Hex + nowISO + mustInt)

src/04_rate_limiter_do.js (RateLimiter DO)

src/05_ledger_do.js (PhoenixLedgerDOv2 DO)

src/06_drones.js (DroneSwarm)

src/07_chat.js (chatProcess + local fallback + later OBI wiring)

src/08_routes.js (router table + protectedPaths + rate-limit gate)

src/index.js (imports + exports default + DO exports)

Now the real issue: your nohup wrangler dev ... & immediately terminated. We need to read why from the log, then restart clean.

Run these commands exactly:



cd ~/phoenix-empire/worker echo "=== last 120 lines of dev.log ===" tail -n 120 dev.log echo "=== wrangler logs directory (latest file) ===" ls -lt ~/.wrangler/logs | head -n 5 echo "=== last 120 lines of newest wrangler log ===" LOG="$(ls -t ~/.wrangler/logs/wrangler-* | head -n 1)" echo "LOG=$LOG" tail -n 120 "$LOG"

While that’s printing, also confirm nothing is already bound to 8787/8788:



ss -ltnp | grep -E ':8787|:8788' || true

Most common causes for “Terminated” in this setup (and the fix):

Port already in use → start on a different port:



nohup npx wrangler dev --local --persist-to .wrangler/state --port 8790 > dev.log 2>&1 &

Wrangler is prompting/interactive and dies under nohup → run without nohup in a dedicated terminal session:



npx wrangler dev --local --persist-to .wrangler/state --port 8787

(or use tmux so it survives disconnects)

Remote-mode warning / wrong flags → ensure you are not accidentally using --remote anywhere (including inside a script).

After you paste the tail output (dev.log + newest wrangler log), I’ll tell you the exact one-line restart command that will stick.

export WORKER_URL="http://127.0.0.1:8790" export SOVEREIGN_KEY="dev-sovereign-key-change-me" echo "== health ==" curl -sS "$WORKER_URL/health"; echo; echo echo "== verifychain (no auth, expect 401) ==" curl -i -sS "$WORKER_URL/verifychain" | head -n 12; echo echo "== verifychain (auth, expect 200) ==" curl -sS "$WORKER_URL/verifychain" -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOVEREIGN_KEY"; echo; echo echo "== append (expect COMMITTED + height++) ==" curl -sS "$WORKER_URL/ledger/append" \ -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOVEREIGN_KEY" \ -H "Content-Type: application/json" \ --data '{"event":"dev_pipeline_append","ts":"now"}'; echo; echo echo "== verifychain again (height incremented) ==" curl -sS "$WORKER_URL/verifychain" -H "x-sovereign-key: $SOVEREIGN_KEY"; echo; echo echo "== chat (LOCAL_MODE_OK) ==" curl -sS "$WORKER_URL/chat" -H "Content-Type: application/json" --data '{"message":"hello"}'; echo

Now make a 10-step pipeline as one script file (no more copy/paste drift). This script will: kill old ports, start 8790, wait until ready, then run the probes.



cat > dev.pipeline.10.sh <<'SH' #!/usr/bin/env bash set -euo pipefail cd ~/phoenix-empire/worker PORT="${PORT:-8790}" WORKER_URL="http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}" SOVEREIGN_KEY="${SOVEREIGN_KEY:-dev-sovereign-key-change-me}" echo "[1/10] Sanity files" ls -la wrangler.toml src/index.js src/chat_endpoint.js .dev.vars >/dev/null echo "[2/10] Stop stray dev servers" pkill -f "wrangler dev" || true pkill -f workerd || true sleep 1 echo "[3/10] Start wrangler dev on port ${PORT}" nohup npx wrangler dev --local --persist-to .wrangler/state --port "${PORT}" > "dev${PORT}.log" 2>&1 & sleep 1 echo "[4/10] Wait for readiness" for i in $(seq 1 40); do if curl -sS "${WORKER_URL}/health" >/dev/null 2>&1; then echo "READY ${WORKER_URL}" break fi sleep 0.25 done echo "[5/10] Health" curl -sS "${WORKER_URL}/health"; echo; echo echo "[6/10] Verifychain no-auth (expect 401)" curl -i -sS "${WORKER_URL}/verifychain" | head -n 12; echo echo "[7/10] Verifychain auth" curl -sS "${WORKER_URL}/verifychain" -H "x-sovereign-key: ${SOVEREIGN_KEY}"; echo; echo echo "[8/10] Append" curl -sS "${WORKER_URL}/ledger/append" \ -H "x-sovereign-key: ${SOVEREIGN_KEY}" \ -H "Content-Type: application/json" \ --data '{"event":"dev_pipeline_append","ts":"now"}' echo; echo echo "[9/10] Verifychain again" curl -sS "${WORKER_URL}/verifychain" -H "x-sovereign-key: ${SOVEREIGN_KEY}"; echo; echo echo "[10/10] Chat (LOCAL_MODE_OK expected if no vendor keys)" curl -sS "${WORKER_URL}/chat" -H "Content-Type: application/json" --data '{"message":"hello"}'; echo echo "PASS: dev pipeline complete. Log: dev${PORT}.log" SH chmod +x dev.pipeline.10.sh

export default {

async fetch(request, env) {

const start = performance.now();

// STEP 19: THE IRON SEAL (IMPROVEMENT 2)

if (!await isValidSovereignWill(sovereign_sig, timestamp, action, env.SOVEREIGN_SECRET)) {

return new Response("UNAUTHORIZED", { status: 403 });

// TWO-PHASE COMMIT (IMPROVEMENT 9)

// STEP 8: THE KINETIC DOWNLINK (IMPROVEMENT 5)

method: 'POST',

headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },

body: JSON.stringify({ action, params, ledger_height: record.height })

});



return new Response(JSON.stringify({

obi: `Reincarnated. ${droneID} is responsive.`,

stone_record: `Height ${record.height}`,

latency: `${(performance.now() - start).toFixed(2)}ms`

}));

}

import time
from flask import Flask
from flask_socketio import SocketIO, emit
from queue import PriorityQueue
# (MAVLink/DroneKit imports assumed)

app = Flask(__name__)
# The Neural Link: threading enabled for sub-10ms context switching
socketio = SocketIO(app, cors_allowed_origins="*", async_mode='threading')

# STEP 9.2: THE JITTER BUFFER (Anti-Lag Memory)
# PriorityQueue ensures 'STOP' (priority 0) jumps ahead of 'MOVE' (priority 1)
intent_buffer = PriorityQueue()

@socketio.on('sovereign_intent')
def handle_intent(payload):
    """
    Direct Neural Reception. 
    Bypasses standard HTTP headers and negotiation.
    """
    action = payload.get('action')
    timestamp = payload.get('timestamp')
    
    # Priority Logic: STOP/HALT gets Priority 0. Everything else is 1.
    priority = 0 if action in ["STOP", "HALT"] else 1
    
    # Push to buffer: (priority, timestamp, payload)
    intent_buffer.put((priority, timestamp, payload))
    
    # 0ms Internal Acknowledgment
    emit('obi_ack', {'status': 'QUEUED', 'ts': time.time()})

def execution_loop():
    """
    The Muscular Reflex. 
    Continuously drains the Jitter Buffer and triggers MAVLink.
    """
    while True:
        if not intent_buffer.empty():

import { sha256Hex, timingSafeEq, nowISO, mustInt } from "./p10/03_crypto.js"; // Standardized helpers

export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const authKey = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key");

    // --- STAGE 6.1: THE AUTHORITY CHAIN ---
    const isSovereign = timingSafeEq(authKey, env.SOVEREIGN_KEY);

    // Public Health & Pulse
    if (url.pathname === "/pulse") {
      return Response.json({ status: "HEARTBEAT_ACTIVE", node: "HCMC_01", version: "v100.6" });
    }

    // --- STAGE 6.2 & 9.1: KINETIC INTENT GATEWAY ---
    if (url.pathname === "/execute" && request.method === "POST") {
      if (!isSovereign) return new Response("ACCESS_DENIED", { status: 401 });

      const payload = await request.json();
      const kineticKeywords = ["RISE", "HALT", "SCAN", "REINCARNATE"];
      const isKinetic = kineticKeywords.includes(payload.action?.toUpperCase());

      // If kinetic, we trigger the Recursive Feedback Loop (6.5)
      if (isKinetic) {
        const ledgerId = env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME");
        const ledger = env.LEDGER.get(ledgerId);
        
        // Commit Intent to Stone BEFORE motor pulse
        const appendRes = await ledger.fetch(new Request("https://ledger/ledger/append", {
          method: "POST",
          body: JSON.stringify({ ...payload, type: "KINETIC_WILL" })
        }));
        const commit = await appendRes.json();

        // P10/05: Recursive Confirmation
        if (commit.ok) {
          // Downlink to Local Hardware (reincarnate_body.py)
          return Response.json({
            obi: `Decree Sealed at Height ${commit.height}. Executing ${payload.action}.`,
            hash: commit.hash,
            execute: true
          });
        }
      }
    }

    // Fallback to Router (src/p10/08_routes.js)
    return env.ROUTER.fetch(request, env);
  }
};

// --- STAGE 7.1: THE MERKLE LEDGER DO ---
export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 {
  constructor(state, env) {
    this.state = state;
    this.storage = state.storage;
    this.sql = state.storage.sql;
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    
    if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append") {
      const data = await request.json();
      const height = (await this.storage.get("height")) || 0;
      const prevHash = (await this.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";
      const nextHeight = height + 1;
      
      // STEP 13.1: SHA-256 Binary Packing
      const ts = nowISO();
      const hash = await sha256Hex(`${nextHeight}:${prevHash}:${ts}:${JSON.stringify(data)}`);
      
      // Commit to SQLite (The Stone Record)
      this.sql.exec(
        "INSERT INTO ledger (height, hash, prev_hash, payload, ts) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
        nextHeight, hash, prevHash, JSON.stringify(data), ts
      );

      await this.storage.put("height", nextHeight);
      await this.storage.put("latest_hash", hash);

      return Response.json({ ok: true, height: nextHeight, hash });
    }
  }
}
